One serial family/configuration at a time; original engines retain cyclic algorithm order. Generation/restoration/query planning and correctness checks stay outside primary steady_clock timing. Sorting, construction and lookup are separate. Instrumentation is absent.

## Results by benchmark family

Cross-dataset rows use equally weighted distinct-input medians after repeated trials are summarized within each dataset. Identical ordered fingerprints collapse to one input unit. Lookup values below are **normalized batch averages**, not individual-query latency. Complete per-dataset statistics/counts remain in summary.json and observations.jsonl.

## Variability and statistical interpretation

All successful measured observations and outliers are retained. Warmups are preserved and excluded from statistics. Sample SD uses n−1 and is unavailable for fewer than two input units/trials. Min–max bars are descriptive spread, not confidence intervals. Repeated timings on one dataset are not independent datasets; seeds/query policies do not establish independent machine sessions. Different source distributions stay separate even when their sorted multisets coincide.

## Known measurement limitations and confounders

Shared Linux machine; CPU frequency, cache, thermal state and background load were not controlled. Validation touches structures before lookup. Nanosecond units do not imply nanosecond accuracy; timer overhead is not subtracted. No direct per-query maxima, isolated structure memory, end-to-end index costs, confidence intervals or significance tests. No comparison or memory figures are fabricated from timing data.

## Reproducibility instructions

Regenerate to new, absent derived paths; never edit raw bundles:
```sh
python3 scripts/analyze_experiment.py /home/ahmedelsheikh/Documents/GitHub/modern-algorithm-benchmark/results/raw/phase5_full_20261009T191000587965Z --output NEW_PROCESSED_DIRECTORY --figures NEW_FIGURE_DIRECTORY
python3 scripts/plot_results.py /home/ahmedelsheikh/Documents/GitHub/modern-algorithm-benchmark/results/raw/phase5_full_20261009T191000587965Z --output NEW_FIGURE_DIRECTORY
```

experiment.json contains exact runner/child commands. observations.jsonl traces every row to its raw CSV; plot_manifest.json records filters/transforms/source hashes/output hashes/plotting versions. Byte identity is checked on the same plotting stack, not promised across versions or platforms.

## Deferred experiments and next steps

Full preset is pending unless this report explicitly identifies a complete full run. Separate count/memory studies, independent-session replication, timer calibration and larger searches require explicit planning. Next roadmap implementation: Phase 6 OpenMP. No CUDA or 100M workload.
